                <h2>Ничего не нашлось</h2>
                <p>Попробуйте короче: «оплата», «хостинг», «запись» или «SEO».</p>
                <p class="mt-16"><a href="{{ 'sitemap'|page }}">Посмотреть карту сайта {{ elv_icon('arrow-right')|raw }}</a></p>
            </div>

            <div class="search-popular" data-search-popular>
                <h2>Частые запросы</h2>
                <div class="chips mt-16">
                    <a class="chip" href="{{ url('/search') }}/?q=оплата">оплата</a>
                    <a class="chip" href="{{ url('/search') }}/?q=хостинг">хостинг</a>
                    <a class="chip" href="{{ url('/search') }}/?q=онлайн-запись">онлайн-запись</a>
                    <a class="chip" href="{{ url('/search') }}/?q=SEO">SEO</a>
                    <a class="chip" href="{{ url('/search') }}/?q=перенос">перенос</a>
                    <a class="chip" href="{{ url('/search') }}/?q=поддержка">поддержка</a>
                </div>
                <p class="muted mt-24">
                    Или откройте <a href="{{ 'sitemap'|page }}">карту сайта</a> — там все разделы и статьи.
                </p>
            </div>

            <noscript>
                <div class="alert alert--warn mt-24" role="status">
                    Поиск работает с JavaScript. Пока можно открыть <a href="{{ 'sitemap'|page }}">карту сайта</a>
                    или <a href="{{ 'blog'|page }}">блог</a>.
                </div>
            </noscript>
        </div>
    </section>
</main>

===== FILE: themes/elv-studio/assets/js/search.js =====

/* Страница поиска: клиентский индекс, чтобы поиск работал и в статической копии. */
(function () {
    'use strict';

    var indexNode = document.querySelector('[data-search-index]');
    var form = document.querySelector('[data-search-form]');
    var input = document.getElementById('site-search-input');
    var results = document.querySelector('[data-search-results]');
    var statusNode = document.querySelector('[data-search-status]');
    var emptyNode = document.querySelector('[data-search-empty]');
    var popularNode = document.querySelector('[data-search-popular]');
    var moreNode = document.querySelector('[data-search-more]');
    var filterNodes = document.querySelectorAll('[data-search-type]');

    if (!indexNode || !form || !input || !results || !statusNode) {
        return;
    }

    var items = [];
    var loaded = false;

    var state = { type: 'all', limit: 30 };

    // Словари — из SearchConfig через data-search-config (единый источник).
    var config = {};

    try {
        config = JSON.parse(indexNode.getAttribute('data-search-config') || '{}') || {};
    } catch (error) {
        config = {};
    }

    var stop = {};

    (config.stop || []).forEach(function (word) {
        stop[word] = 1;
    });

    var synonymGroups = config.synonyms || [];
    var endings = config.endings || [];
    var timer = null;

    function normalize(value) {
        var text = String(value || '').toLowerCase().replace(/ё/g, 'е');

        return text.match(/[\p{L}\p{Nd}]+/gu) || [];
    }

    function phrase(value) {
        return normalize(value).join(' ');
    }

    function words(value) {
        var all = normalize(value);
        var seen = {};
        var list = [];

        for (var i = 0; i < all.length; i += 1) {
            var word = all[i];

            if (word.length < 2 || stop[word] || seen[word]) {
                continue;
            }

            seen[word] = true;
            list.push(word);
        }

        return list;
    }

    function stem(word) {
        if (word.length <= 3) {
            return word;
        }

        for (var i = 0; i < endings.length; i += 1) {
            var ending = endings[i];
            var value = word.slice(0, -ending.length);

            if (word.slice(-ending.length) === ending && value.length >= 3) {
                return value;
            }
        }

        return word;
    }

    function formsFor(word) {
        var list = [word];
        var value = stem(word);

        if (value !== word) {
            list.push(value);
        }

        for (var i = 0; i < synonymGroups.length; i += 1) {
            var group = synonymGroups[i];

            if (group.indexOf(word) === -1) {
                continue;
            }

            group.forEach(function (item) {
                list.push(item);

                var itemStem = stem(item);

                if (itemStem !== item) {
                    list.push(itemStem);
                }
            });

            break;
        }

        return list.filter(function (item, index, all) {
            return item.length >= 2 && all.indexOf(item) === index;
        });
    }

    function escapeRegExp(value) {
        return value.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
    }

    function hasForm(text, value) {
        // Как в Search::containsAny(): начало слова проверяем всегда, конец —
        // только для коротких форм, иначе «став» находил бы «доставку».
        var suffix = value.length < 4 ? '(?![\\p{L}\\p{N}])' : '';

        return new RegExp('(^|[^\\p{L}\\p{N}])' + escapeRegExp(value) + suffix, 'u').test(text);
    }

    function hasAny(text, forms) {
        for (var i = 0; i < forms.length; i += 1) {
            if (hasForm(text, forms[i])) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    function score(item, query, parts) {
        var title = phrase(item.title);
        var description = phrase(item.description);
        var text = phrase(item.text);
        var keywords = phrase(item.keywords);
        var meta = phrase(item.meta);
        var total = title.indexOf(query) !== -1 ? 14 : 0;
        var hits = 0;
        var wordForms = parts.map(formsFor);

        parts.forEach(function (word, index) {
            var forms = wordForms[index];
            var hit = false;

            if (hasAny(title, forms)) {
                total += 7;
                hit = true;
            }

            if (hasAny(keywords, forms)) {
                total += 5;
                hit = true;
            }

            if (hasAny(description, forms)) {
                total += 3;
                hit = true;
            }

            if (hasAny(text, forms)) {
                total += 2;
                hit = true;
            }

            if (hasAny(meta, forms)) {
                total += 1;
                hit = true;
            }

            if (hit) {
                hits += 1;
            }
        });

        if (hits === 0) {
            return 0;
        }

        if (parts.length > 1) {
            total += hits * 2;
            total -= (parts.length - hits) * 2;
        }

        return total;
    }

    function plural(count, one, few, many) {
        var last = count % 10;
        var lastTwo = count % 100;

        if (last === 1 && lastTwo !== 11) {
            return one;
        }

        if (last >= 2 && last <= 4 && (lastTwo < 10 || lastTwo >= 20)) {
            return few;
        }

        return many;
    }

    function appendHighlighted(node, value, parts) {
        var source = String(value || '');
        var forms = [];

        parts.forEach(function (word) {
            forms = forms.concat(formsFor(word));
        });

        forms = forms.filter(function (form, index, all) {
            return form.length >= 3 && all.indexOf(form) === index;
        });

        if (!forms.length) {
            node.textContent = source;
            return;
        }

        forms.sort(function (a, b) {
            return b.length - a.length;
        });

        var longForms = forms.filter(function (form) {
            return form.length >= 4;
        });
        var shortForms = forms.filter(function (form) {
            return form.length === 3;
        });
        var chunks = [];

        if (longForms.length) {
            chunks.push('(' + longForms.map(escapeRegExp).join('|') + ')');
        }

        if (shortForms.length) {
            // Короткие аббревиатуры (SEO, PHP, SSL) подсвечиваем только целым
            // словом: иначе «api» попадёт внутрь «капитал».
            chunks.push('(?:^|[^\\p{L}\\p{N}])(' + shortForms.map(escapeRegExp).join('|') + ')(?![\\p{L}\\p{N}])');
        }

        var pattern = new RegExp(chunks.join('|'), 'giu');
        var last = 0;
        var match;

        while ((match = pattern.exec(source)) !== null) {
            var hit = match[1] || match[2] || '';
            var start = match.index + (match[1] ? 0 : match[0].length - hit.length);

            if (start > last) {
                node.appendChild(document.createTextNode(source.slice(last, start)));
            }

            var mark = document.createElement('mark');
            mark.textContent = hit;
            node.appendChild(mark);
            last = start + hit.length;

            if (match[0] === '') {
                pattern.lastIndex += 1;
            }
        }

        if (last < source.length) {
            node.appendChild(document.createTextNode(source.slice(last)));
        }
    }

    function card(item, parts) {
        var link = document.createElement('a');
        link.className = 'search-result';
        link.href = item.url;

        var top = document.createElement('div');
        top.className = 'search-result__top';

        var type = document.createElement('span');
        type.className = 'search-result__type';
        type.textContent = item.type === 'blog' ? 'Статья' : 'Страница';
        top.appendChild(type);

        if (item.group) {
            var group = document.createElement('span');
            group.className = 'search-result__group';
            group.textContent = item.group;
            top.appendChild(group);
        }

        link.appendChild(top);

        var title = document.createElement('h2');
        appendHighlighted(title, item.title || '', parts);
        link.appendChild(title);

        var description = item.description || item.text || '';
        if (description) {
            var lead = document.createElement('p');
            appendHighlighted(lead, description, parts);
            link.appendChild(lead);
        }

        if (item.meta) {
            var meta = document.createElement('span');
            meta.className = 'search-result__meta';
            meta.textContent = item.meta;
            link.appendChild(meta);
        }

        return link;
    }

    function render() {
        if (!loaded) {
            statusNode.textContent = 'Загружаем поиск…';
            return;
        }

        var raw = input.value.trim();
        var query = phrase(raw);
        var parts = words(raw);

        results.textContent = '';
        results.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'false');
        emptyNode.hidden = true;

        if (!raw) {
            popularNode.hidden = false;

            if (moreNode) {
                moreNode.hidden = true;
            }

            statusNode.textContent = 'Введите запрос — покажем страницы и статьи.';
            return;
        }

        popularNode.hidden = true;
        results.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');

        if (!parts.length) {
            if (moreNode) {
                moreNode.hidden = true;
            }

            results.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'false');
            statusNode.textContent = 'Введите хотя бы два символа.';
            return;
        }

        var found = [];

        items.forEach(function (item) {
            if (state.type !== 'all' && item.type !== state.type) {
                return;
            }

            var value = score(item, query, parts);

            if (value > 0) {
                found.push({ item: item, score: value });
            }
        });

        found.sort(function (a, b) {
            if (a.score === b.score) {
                return String(a.item.title).localeCompare(String(b.item.title), 'ru');
            }

            return b.score - a.score;
        });

        if (!found.length) {
            if (moreNode) {
                moreNode.hidden = true;
            }

            results.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'false');
            emptyNode.hidden = false;
            statusNode.textContent = 'Ничего не нашлось по запросу «' + raw + '».';
            return;
        }

        var shown = found.slice(0, state.limit);
        var suffix = plural(found.length, 'результат', 'результата', 'результатов');
        statusNode.textContent = found.length > shown.length
            ? 'Нашли ' + found.length + ' ' + suffix + ', показываем первые ' + shown.length + '.'
            : 'Нашли ' + found.length + ' ' + suffix + '.';

        shown.forEach(function (entry) {
            results.appendChild(card(entry.item, parts));
        });

        if (moreNode) {
            moreNode.hidden = found.length <= shown.length;
        }

        results.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'false');
    }

    function syncUrl() {
        var value = input.value.trim();
        var base = window.location.pathname.replace(/index\.html$/, '');

        if (base.charAt(base.length - 1) !== '/') {
            base += '/';
        }

        try {
            window.history.replaceState(null, '', base + (value ? '?q=' + encodeURIComponent(value) : ''));
        } catch (error) {
            /* file:// не даёт менять адрес — поиск всё равно работает. */
        }
    }

    input.addEventListener('input', function () {
        state.limit = 30;
        window.clearTimeout(timer);
        timer = window.setTimeout(function () {
            syncUrl();
            render();
        }, 120);
    });

    form.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        state.limit = 30;
        syncUrl();
        render();
    });

    Array.prototype.forEach.call(filterNodes, function (button) {
        button.addEventListener('click', function () {
            state.type = button.getAttribute('data-search-type') || 'all';
            state.limit = 30;

            Array.prototype.forEach.call(filterNodes, function (node) {
                node.classList.toggle('chip--active', node === button);
            });

            render();
        });
    });

    if (moreNode) {
        moreNode.addEventListener('click', function () {
            state.limit += 30;
            render();
        });
    }

    statusNode.textContent = 'Загружаем поиск…';

    var initial = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('q');

    if (initial && !input.value) {
        input.value = initial;
    }

    fetch(indexNode.getAttribute('data-search-index') || '/search-index.json', {
        headers: { Accept: 'application/json' }
    })
        .then(function (response) {
            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error('search index ' + response.status);
            }

            return response.json();
        })
        .then(function (data) {
            items = Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
            loaded = true;

            if (input.value.trim()) {
                render();
                return;
            }

            statusNode.textContent = 'Введите запрос — покажем страницы и статьи.';
            popularNode.hidden = false;
        })
        .catch(function () {
            statusNode.textContent = 'Поиск временно недоступен. Откройте карту сайта.';
        });
})();

===== FILE: themes/elv-studio/assets/css/search.css =====

# Карта сайта и SEO-разметка

Срез: 24.11.2026. Stage 86. Коммит: `1a403ae`.

===== FILE: themes/elv-studio/pages/sitemap.htm =====

title = "Карта сайта"
url = "/sitemap/"
layout = "default"
meta_title = "Карта сайта"
meta_description = "Все разделы сайта Elv Kit: возможности, цены, демо, инструкция, услуги, блог, контакты и правовые документы. Быстрый переход в нужный раздел."

{% set crumbs = elv_breadcrumbs() %}
{% set categories = elv_categories('all') %}
{% set latest = elv_posts(6, null, 'all') %}
{% set totalPosts = elv_blog_total('all') %}

<main id="main">
    <section class="page-hero">
        <div class="container">
            {% partial 'breadcrumbs' crumbs=crumbs %}
            <span class="eyebrow">Навигация</span>
            <h1>Карта сайта</h1>
            <p class="lead">Все разделы в одном месте: начните с возможностей и цен, выберите услугу или найдите ответ в блоге. Машиночитаемая версия — <a href="{{ url('/sitemap.xml') }}">sitemap.xml</a>, лента статей — <a href="{{ url('/rss.xml') }}">RSS</a>.</p>

            <nav class="chips mt-24" aria-label="Разделы карты сайта">
                <a class="chip" href="#sitemap-site">Сайт</a>
                <a class="chip" href="#sitemap-services">Услуги</a>
                <a class="chip" href="#sitemap-modules">Модули</a>
                <a class="chip" href="#sitemap-buy">Купить и связаться</a>
                <a class="chip" href="#sitemap-legal">Правовое</a>
                <a class="chip" href="#sitemap-blog">Блог</a>
            </nav>
        </div>
    </section>

    <section class="section">
        <div class="container">
            <div class="grid grid--3">
                <div class="card">
                    <h2 id="sitemap-site">Сайт</h2>
                    <ul class="mt-24">
                        <li><a href="{{ 'index'|page }}">Главная</a></li>

===== FILE: plugins/elv/kit/classes/Seo.php =====

<?php namespace Elv\Kit\Classes;

use Cms\Classes\Page;
use Cms\Classes\Theme;
use Elv\Kit\Models\Post;
use Elv\Kit\Models\Product;
use Elv\Kit\Models\Settings;

/**
 * Seo — мета, canonical, микроразметка и директивы обхода.
 *
 * Числа и ссылки собираются из БД и файлов, а не из констант: карта сайта
 * обязана совпадать с тем, что реально отвечает 200.
 */
class Seo
{
    public const SECTIONS = [
        'kit' => 'Возможности',
        'template' => 'Шаблон',
        'modules' => 'Разделы',
        'services' => 'Услуги',
        'calculator' => 'Калькулятор',
        'pricing' => 'Цены',
        'demo' => 'Демо',
        'blog' => 'Блог',
        'docs' => 'Инструкция',
        'order' => 'Заказать',
        'booking' => 'Запись на показ',
        'account' => 'Личный кабинет',
        'tickets' => 'Поддержка',
        'feedback' => 'Обратная связь',
        'contacts' => 'Контакты',
        'terms' => 'Оферта',
        'privacy' => 'Политика конфиденциальности',
        'sitemap' => 'Карта сайта',
        'search' => 'Поиск',
    ];

    protected static $post = null;

    protected static $product = null;

    public static function siteName(): string
    {
        return (string) (Settings::get('brand_name') ?: 'Elv Kit');
    }

    public static function tagline(): string
    {
        return (string) (Settings::get('brand_tagline') ?: 'Готовый сайт для бизнеса с онлайн-записью и оплатой');
    }

    public static function base(): string
    {
        return rtrim(url('/'), '/');
    }

    public static function canonical(?int $pageNo = null): string
    {
        $path = '/' . ltrim((string) request()->path(), '/');

        if ($path !== '/') {
            $path = rtrim($path, '/') . '/';
        }

        $url = static::base() . $path;

        if ($pageNo !== null && $pageNo > 1) {
            $url .= '?page=' . $pageNo;
        }

        return $url;
    }

    public static function setPost(?Post $post): void
    {
        static::$post = $post;
    }

    public static function post(): ?Post
    {
        return static::$post;
    }

    public static function setProduct(?Product $product): void
    {
        static::$product = $product;
    }

    public static function product(): ?Product
    {
        return static::$product;
    }

    /**
     * title — заголовок страницы с суффиксом бренда.
     */
    public static function title($page, ?int $pageNo = null): string
    {
        $suffix = ($pageNo !== null && $pageNo > 1) ? ' — страница ' . $pageNo : '';

        if (static::$post) {
            return ($page->meta_title ?: static::$post->seo_title ?: static::$post->title) . ' — ' . static::siteName();
        }

        $brand = static::siteName();
        $raw = trim((string) ($page->meta_title ?? $page->title ?? ''));

        if ($raw === '') {
            return $brand . $suffix;
        }

        if (str_contains($raw, $brand)) {
            return $raw . $suffix;
        }

        return $raw . $suffix . ' — ' . $brand;
    }

    public static function description($page): string
    {
        $value = trim((string) ($page->meta_description ?? ''));

        if ($value === '' && static::$post) {
            $value = trim((string) (static::$post->seo_description ?: strip_tags((string) static::$post->excerpt)));
        }

        if ($value === '') {
            $value = (string) (Settings::get('brand_description') ?: static::tagline());
        }

        return mb_substr($value, 0, 300);
    }

    public static function image($page = null): string
    {
        $fallback = static::base() . '/themes/elv-studio/assets/img/og-default.png';
        $cover = static::$post ? trim((string) static::$post->cover) : '';

        // Обложка статьи — имя иконки (graph, calendar, spark), а не картинка.
        // Ссылку берём, только если в поле действительно путь к изображению.
        if ($cover === '' || preg_match('~\.(png|jpe?g|webp|gif|svg)$~i', $cover) !== 1) {
            return $fallback;
        }

        if (preg_match('~^https?://~i', $cover) === 1) {
            return $cover;
        }

        return static::base() . '/' . ltrim($cover, '/');
    }

    /**
     * breadcrumbs — видимые крошки и BreadcrumbList из одного источника.
     *
     * @return array<int, array{name: string, url: string}>
     */
    public static function breadcrumbs(): array
    {
        $segments = array_values(array_filter(explode('/', trim((string) request()->path(), '/'))));

        if ($segments === []) {
            return [];
        }

        $crumbs = [['name' => 'Главная', 'url' => static::base() . '/']];

        if (!isset(static::SECTIONS[$segments[0]])) {
            return $crumbs;
        }

        $crumbs[] = [
            'name' => static::SECTIONS[$segments[0]],
            'url' => static::base() . '/' . $segments[0] . '/',
        ];

        if (count($segments) > 1) {
            if ($segments[1] === 'tech' && !static::$post) {
                $crumbs[] = [
                    'name' => 'Технический раздел',
                    'url' => static::base() . '/blog/tech/',
                ];

                return $crumbs;
            }

            if (static::$post && static::$post->audience === Post::AUDIENCE_TECH) {
                $crumbs[] = [
                    'name' => 'Технический раздел',
                    'url' => static::base() . '/blog/tech/',
                ];
            }

            $name = static::slugTitle($segments[1]);
            $crumbs[] = ['name' => $name, 'url' => static::canonical()];
        }

        return $crumbs;
    }

    /**
     * slugTitle — подпись последней крошки по данным, а не по адресу.
     */
    protected static function slugTitle(string $slug): string
    {
        if (static::$post && static::$post->slug === $slug) {
            return (string) static::$post->title;
        }

        $product = Product::query()->where('slug', $slug)->first();

        if ($product) {
            return (string) $product->name;
        }

        $sections = [
            'hosting' => 'Хостинг сайта',
            'support' => 'Поддержка сайта',
            'plugins' => 'Плагины для October CMS',
            'booking' => 'Сайт с онлайн-записью',
            'audit' => 'Бесплатный аудит сайта',
            'seo' => 'SEO-продвижение сайта',
        ];

        if (isset($sections[$slug])) {
            return $sections[$slug];
        }

        return ucfirst(str_replace('-', ' ', $slug));
    }

    /**
     * jsonld — @graph с организацией, сайтом и узлом страницы.
     */
    public static function jsonld($page): string
    {
        $base = static::base();

        $graph = [
            [
                '@type' => 'Organization',
                '@id' => $base . '/#organization',
                'name' => static::siteName(),
                'url' => $base . '/',
                'logo' => $base . '/themes/elv-studio/assets/img/og-default.png',
                'email' => (string) Settings::get('email'),
                'sameAs' => array_values(array_filter([
                    (string) Settings::get('telegram'),
                    (string) Settings::get('github_url'),
                ])),
            ],
            [
                '@type' => 'WebSite',
                '@id' => $base . '/#website',
                'url' => $base . '/',
                'name' => static::siteName(),
                'inLanguage' => 'ru',
                'publisher' => ['@id' => $base . '/#organization'],
                'potentialAction' => [
                    '@type' => 'SearchAction',
                    'target' => [
                        '@type' => 'EntryPoint',
                        'urlTemplate' => $base . '/search/?q={search_term_string}',
                    ],
                    'query-input' => 'required name=search_term_string',
                ],
            ],
        ];

        $pageId = (string) ($page->id ?? $page->getBaseFileName() ?? '');

        if ($pageId === 'index') {
            $graph[] = static::softwareNode();
            $graph[] = static::faqNode(null, 'faq', Faq::HOME_LIMIT);
        }

        if ($pageId === 'pricing') {
            $graph[] = static::faqNode(static::canonical() . '#pricing-faq');
        }

        if ($pageId === 'calculator') {
            $graph[] = static::faqNode(static::canonical() . '#calculator-faq', 'calculator');
        }

        if ($pageId === 'services') {
            $graph[] = static::faqNode(static::canonical() . '#services-faq', 'services');
        }

        if ($pageId === 'services-seo') {
            $graph[] = static::faqNode(static::canonical() . '#seo-faq', 'seo');
        }

        if ($pageId === 'kit') {
            $graph[] = static::softwareNode();
        }

        if (static::$post) {
            $graph[] = static::articleNode(static::$post);

            $postFaq = Faq::post((string) static::$post->slug);

            if ($postFaq !== []) {
                $graph[] = static::faqNodeFromItems(static::canonical() . '#post-faq', $postFaq);
            }
        }

        if (static::$product) {
            $graph[] = static::productNode(static::$product);

            $qa = static::qaNode(static::$product);

            if ($qa !== []) {
                $graph[] = $qa;
            }
        }

        $crumbs = static::breadcrumbs();

        if (count($crumbs) > 1) {
            $items = [];

            foreach ($crumbs as $index => $crumb) {
                $items[] = [
                    '@type' => 'ListItem',
                    'position' => $index + 1,
                    'name' => $crumb['name'],
                    'item' => $crumb['url'],
                ];
            }

            $graph[] = [
                '@type' => 'BreadcrumbList',
                '@id' => static::canonical() . '#breadcrumb',
                'itemListElement' => $items,
            ];
        }

        $graph = array_values(array_filter($graph));

        $json = json_encode(
            ['@context' => 'https://schema.org', '@graph' => $graph],
            JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES
        );

        return $json === false ? '' : str_replace('</', '<\/', $json);
    }

    protected static function softwareNode(): array
    {
        $kit = Product::kit();

        if (!$kit) {
            return [];
        }

        // Цены — из версий прайса (Prices::resolve внутри Catalog::plans),
        // а не из карточки товара: иначе микроразметка расходится с /pricing/.
        $prices = [];

        foreach (Catalog::plans($kit) as $plan) {
            $price = (int) ($plan['price'] ?? 0);

            if ($price > 0) {
                $prices[] = $price;
            }
        }

        $offers = null;

        if ($prices !== []) {
            $offers = [
                '@type' => 'AggregateOffer',
                'lowPrice' => (string) (min($prices) / 100),
                'highPrice' => (string) (max($prices) / 100),
                'offerCount' => (string) count($prices),
                'priceCurrency' => Money::currency(),
                'url' => static::base() . '/pricing/',
                'availability' => 'https://schema.org/InStock',
            ];
        }

        return static::withoutEmpty([
            '@type' => 'SoftwareApplication',
            '@id' => static::base() . '/kit/#software',
            'name' => static::siteName(),
            'applicationCategory' => 'BusinessApplication',
            'operatingSystem' => 'Web',
            'description' => (string) (Settings::get('brand_description') ?: static::tagline()),
            'url' => static::base() . '/kit/',
            'offers' => $offers,
            'featureList' => $kit && is_array($kit->features) ? array_values($kit->features) : [],
        ]);
    }

    protected static function faqNode(?string $id = null, string $source = 'faq', ?int $limit = null): array
    {
        return static::faqNodeFromItems($id, Faq::items($source, $limit));
    }

    protected static function faqNodeFromItems(?string $id, array $source): array
    {
        $items = [];

        foreach ($source as $item) {
            if (!isset($item['q'], $item['a'])) {
                continue;
            }

            $items[] = [
                '@type' => 'Question',
                'name' => $item['q'],
                'acceptedAnswer' => ['@type' => 'Answer', 'text' => $item['a']],
            ];
        }

        if ($items === []) {
            return [];
        }

        return ['@type' => 'FAQPage', '@id' => $id ?: (static::base() . '/#faq'), 'mainEntity' => $items];
    }

    protected static function productNode(Product $product): array
    {
        $node = static::withoutEmpty([
            '@type' => 'Product',
            '@id' => static::canonical() . '#product',
            'name' => (string) $product->name,
            'description' => (string) ($product->description ?: $product->tagline),
            'sku' => (string) $product->slug,
            'brand' => ['@type' => 'Brand', 'name' => static::siteName()],
            'url' => static::canonical(),
        ]);

        $node['offers'] = [
            '@type' => 'Offer',
            'price' => (string) (max(0, Prices::resolve($product, 'single')) / 100),
            'priceCurrency' => Money::currency(),
            'url' => static::canonical(),
            'availability' => 'https://schema.org/InStock',
        ];

        // Рейтинг — только по одобренным отзывам: выдуманная оценка в
        // микроразметке расходится с видимым блоком и является дефектом.
        $aggregate = Reviews::aggregate($product);

        if ($aggregate !== null) {
            $node['aggregateRating'] = $aggregate;
        }

        return $node;
    }

    /**
     * qaNode — вопросы и ответы продукта.
     *
     * Один вопрос — `QAPage` (страница отвечает на один вопрос), несколько —
     * `FAQPage`: так разметка соответствует тому, что видит посетитель.
     */
    protected static function qaNode(Product $product): array
    {
        $items = [];

        foreach (Questions::published($product, 20) as $question) {
            $items[] = [
                '@type' => 'Question',
                'name' => (string) $question->question,
                'text' => (string) $question->question,
                'answerCount' => 1,
                'acceptedAnswer' => static::withoutEmpty([
                    '@type' => 'Answer',
                    'text' => (string) $question->answer,
                    'dateCreated' => $question->answered_at ? $question->answered_at->format('c') : null,
                ]),
                'author' => ['@type' => 'Person', 'name' => (string) $question->display_name],
            ];
        }

        if ($items === []) {
            return [];
        }

        return [
            '@type' => count($items) === 1 ? 'QAPage' : 'FAQPage',
            '@id' => static::canonical() . '#qa',
            'mainEntity' => count($items) === 1 ? $items[0] : $items,
        ];
    }

    protected static function articleNode(Post $post): array
    {
        return static::withoutEmpty([
            '@type' => 'BlogPosting',
            '@id' => static::canonical() . '#article',
            'headline' => (string) $post->title,
            'description' => (string) ($post->seo_description ?: strip_tags((string) $post->excerpt)),
            'datePublished' => $post->iso_date,
            'dateModified' => $post->updated_at ? $post->updated_at->format('c') : null,
            'author' => ['@type' => 'Person', 'name' => (string) ($post->author ?: static::siteName())],
            'publisher' => ['@id' => static::base() . '/#organization'],
            'mainEntityOfPage' => ['@type' => 'WebPage', '@id' => static::canonical()],
            'url' => static::canonical(),
            'image' => static::image(),
            'wordCount' => static::articleWordCount($post),
            'keywords' => implode(', ', $post->tag_list),
            'articleSection' => $post->category ? (string) $post->category->name : (string) $post->audience_label,
        ]);
    }

    protected static function articleWordCount(Post $post): int
    {
        $matches = [];
        preg_match_all('/[\p{L}\p{N}]+/u', strip_tags((string) $post->body), $matches);

        return count($matches[0] ?? []);
    }

    /**
     * robots — Google + Яндекс: служебные разделы закрыты, фильтры не плодят страницы.
     */
    public static function robots(): string
    {
        return implode("\n", [
            '# robots.txt — ' . static::siteName(),
            '',
            'User-agent: *',
            'Allow: /',
            'Disallow: /admin/',
            'Disallow: /api/',
            'Disallow: /account/',
            'Disallow: /tickets/',
            'Disallow: /review/',
            'Disallow: /order/success/',
            'Disallow: /download/',
            'Disallow: /*?q=',
            'Disallow: /*?category=',
            'Disallow: /*?tag=',
            '',
            '# Яндекс: параметры фильтров не создают новых страниц',
            'Clean-param: q&category&tag',
            '',
            'User-agent: Yandex',
            'Allow: /',
            'Disallow: /admin/',
            '',
            'Sitemap: ' . static::base() . '/sitemap.xml',
            '',
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * llms — карта сайта для ИИ-краулеров и AI-режима Google.
     */
    public static function llms(): string
    {
        $kit = Product::kit();
        $kitPrice = $kit ? Prices::resolve($kit, 'single') : 0;

        $lines = [
            '# ' . static::siteName(),
            '',
            '> ' . static::tagline(),
            '',
            (string) (Settings::get('brand_description') ?: ''),
            '',
            '## Что это',
            '- [Elv Kit](' . static::base() . '/kit/): готовый сайт для бизнеса — услуги, онлайн-запись, заказы и оплата, блог и админка' . ($kitPrice > 0 ? '; цена от ' . Money::format($kitPrice) : '') . '.',
            '- [Цены](' . static::base() . '/pricing/): один сайт, до 5 сайтов, без ограничений. Разделы можно купить отдельно.',
            '- [Разделы](' . static::base() . '/modules/): онлайн-запись, заказы и оплата, обратная связь, блог.',
            '- [Калькулятор](' . static::base() . '/calculator/): лицензия и модули, три суммы и разбивка без скрытых платежей.',
            '- [Услуги](' . static::base() . '/services/): хостинг, поддержка, плагины, онлайн-запись, SEO и бесплатный аудит.',
            '- [SEO-продвижение](' . static::base() . '/services/seo/): техническая база, структура, контент и аналитика без обещаний «топ-1».',
            '',
            '## Помощь',
            '- [Инструкция](' . static::base() . '/docs/): запуск на своём домене по шагам, без команд.',
            '- [Демо](' . static::base() . '/demo/): как выглядит сайт и админка.',
            '',
            '## Блог для бизнеса',
        ];

        foreach (Post::query()->published()->audience(Post::AUDIENCE_BUSINESS)->limit(10)->get() as $post) {
            $lines[] = '- [' . $post->title . '](' . $post->url . '): ' . trim(strip_tags((string) $post->excerpt));
        }

        $lines[] = '';
        $lines[] = '## Технический раздел';
        $lines[] = '- [Разборы для разработчиков](' . static::base() . '/blog/tech/): скорость, миграции, оплата, бронирование и SEO.';

        foreach (Post::query()->published()->audience(Post::AUDIENCE_TECH)->limit(5)->get() as $post) {
            $lines[] = '- [' . $post->title . '](' . $post->url . '): ' . trim(strip_tags((string) $post->excerpt));
        }

        $lines[] = '';
        $lines[] = '## Контакты';
        $lines[] = '- E-mail: ' . (string) Settings::get('email');
        $lines[] = '- Telegram: ' . ((string) Settings::get('telegram') ?: '—');
        $lines[] = '';

        return implode("\n", $lines);
    }

    protected static function withoutEmpty(array $data): array
    {
        $result = [];

        foreach ($data as $key => $value) {
            if (is_array($value)) {
                $value = static::withoutEmpty($value);
            }

            if ($value === '' || $value === null || $value === []) {
                continue;
            }

            $result[$key] = $value;
        }

        return $result;
    }
}

===== FILE: plugins/elv/kit/classes/Sitemap.php =====

<?php namespace Elv\Kit\Classes;

use Cms\Classes\Page;
use Cms\Classes\Theme;
use Elv\Kit\Models\Post;
use Elv\Kit\Models\Product;
use Elv\Kit\Models\Settings;

/**
 * Sitemap — карта сайта из живых страниц темы и записей БД.
 */
class Sitemap
{
    public static function xml(): string
    {
        $urls = [];
        $base = Seo::base();

        foreach (static::pages() as $page) {
            $urls[static::key((string) $page['loc'])] = $page['lastmod'];
        }

        foreach (Product::query()->active()->get() as $product) {
            $urls[static::key((string) $product->url)] = optional($product->updated_at)->format('c') ?: now()->format('c');
        }

        foreach (Post::query()->published()->get() as $post) {
            $urls[static::key((string) $post->url)] = optional($post->updated_at)->format('c') ?: now()->format('c');
        }

        ksort($urls);

        $lines = [
            '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>',
            '<urlset xmlns="http://www.sitemaps.org/schemas/sitemap/0.9">',
        ];

        foreach ($urls as $loc => $lastmod) {
            if (!str_starts_with((string) $loc, 'http')) {
                $loc = $base . '/' . ltrim((string) $loc, '/');
            }

            $lines[] = '  <url>';
            $lines[] = '    <loc>' . htmlspecialchars((string) $loc, ENT_XML1) . '</loc>';

            if ((string) $lastmod !== '') {
                $lines[] = '    <lastmod>' . htmlspecialchars((string) $lastmod, ENT_XML1) . '</lastmod>';
            }

            $lines[] = '  </url>';
        }

        $lines[] = '</urlset>';

        return implode("\n", $lines);
    }

    /**
     * key — каноническая форма адреса: со слэшем, как canonical и ссылки сайта.
     * Лишние слэши срезаются, один ставится обратно, поэтому /blog и /blog/
     * не попадают в карту дважды.
     */
    protected static function key(string $loc): string
    {
        return rtrim($loc, '/') . '/';
    }

    /**
     * pages — статические страницы темы. Шаблоны с параметрами (/blog/:slug)
     * в карту не попадают: их адреса приходят из БД.
     */
    public static function pages(): array
    {
        $result = [];

        try {
            $theme = Theme::getActiveTheme();
            $pages = Page::listInTheme($theme, true);
        }
        catch (\Throwable $e) {
            return $result;
        }

        foreach ($pages as $page) {
            $url = trim((string) ($page->url ?? ''));

            if ($url === '' || str_contains($url, ':') || str_contains($url, '404')) {
                continue;
            }

            if (!empty($page->hidden)) {
                continue;
            }

            if (str_contains(strtolower((string) ($page->meta_robots ?? '')), 'noindex')) {
                continue;
            }

            $result[] = [
                'loc' => Seo::base() . '/' . ltrim($url, '/'),
                'lastmod' => static::pageLastmod(),
            ];
        }

        return $result;
    }

    /**
     * pageLastmod — дата контента, а не filemtime: у файла на новом сервере
     * дата равна распаковке архива, и sitemap врал бы об обновлении страницы.
     * Владелец задаёт дату в настройках; пусто — lastmod не выводится.
     */
    protected static function pageLastmod(): string
    {
        $value = trim((string) Settings::get('sitemap_lastmod'));

        if ($value === '') {
            return '';
        }

        try {
            return \Carbon\Carbon::parse($value)->startOfDay()->format('c');
        }
        catch (\Throwable $e) {
            return '';
        }
    }
}

<?php

 * сессии: подпись шлюза и есть аутентификация, а CSRF-токена у Stripe нет.

        ->where('id', '[0-9]+')

        ->where('id', '[0-9]+')
        ->name('elv.kit.ticket.close');

# Проверки, доки и аудит приватности

Срез: 24.11.2026. Stage 86. Коммит: `1a403ae`.

===== FILE: tools/chat-check.php =====

<?php

require __DIR__ . '/../bootstrap/autoload.php';

$app = require __DIR__ . '/../bootstrap/app.php';
$app->make(Illuminate\Contracts\Console\Kernel::class)->bootstrap();

use Elv\Kit\Classes\Chat;
use Elv\Kit\Classes\TextFilter;
use Elv\Kit\Models\Settings;

$errors = [];

$check = function (bool $condition, string $message) use (&$errors) {
    if (!$condition) {
        $errors[] = $message;
    }
};

foreach (['хуй', 'х у й', 'xyй', 'пиззда', 'fuck', 'п1зд', '3бал', 'д0лбоеб', 'дро4', 'хуйня'] as $word) {
    $check(TextFilter::has($word), 'filter misses: ' . $word);
}

foreach (['хутор', 'нормальный вопрос', 'установка', 'х0р', 'п1тон', 'эпидраздел'] as $word) {
    $check(!TextFilter::has($word), 'filter false positive: ' . $word);
}

foreach (['shitake', 'dickens', 'pussycat', 'cocktail'] as $word) {
    $check(!TextFilter::has($word), 'filter latin false positive: ' . $word);
}

foreach (['fuck', 'fucking', 'bitches', 'dicks', 'xyй', 'х у й'] as $word) {
    $check(TextFilter::has($word), 'filter latin/cyrillic miss: ' . $word);
}

$refusal = Chat::answer('хуй');
$check($refusal['answer'] === TextFilter::REFUSAL, 'profanity refusal is not returned');
$check($refusal['sources'] === [], 'profanity refusal must not expose search sources');
$check($refusal['rejected'] === true, 'profanity message must be rejected');
$check(($refusal['hide_user'] ?? false) === true, 'profanity must hide the user bubble');

foreach (['абырвалг', 'фывап', 'абырвалг абырвалг', 'абырвалг оплата', 'фывап тарифы'] as $nonsense) {
    $reply = Chat::answer($nonsense);
    $check($reply['rejected'] === true, 'nonsense message is not rejected: ' . $nonsense);
    $check(str_contains($reply['answer'], 'Не понял'), 'nonsense answer is missing: ' . $nonsense);
    $check($reply['sources'] === [], 'nonsense answer must not expose sources: ' . $nonsense);
    $check(($reply['hide_user'] ?? false) === false, 'nonsense must keep the user bubble: ' . $nonsense);
}

foreach (['nginx', 'webhook', 'stripe', 'телеграм', 'вебхук', 'как настроить nginx', 'stripe оплата'] as $term) {
    $reply = Chat::answer($term);
    $check($reply['rejected'] === false, 'technical term is rejected: ' . $term);
    $check(($reply['hide_user'] ?? false) === false, 'technical term hides the user bubble: ' . $term);
}

$telegram = Chat::answer('телеграм');
$check(
    mb_stripos($telegram['answer'], 'e-mail') !== false
    || mb_stripos($telegram['answer'], 'telegram') !== false
    || mb_stripos($telegram['answer'], 'почт') !== false,
    'telegram request does not return contacts'
);

$crowdfunding = Chat::answer('краудфандинг');
$check($crowdfunding['rejected'] === false, 'known off-site term is rejected: краудфандинг');

$empty = Chat::answer('');
$check(str_contains($empty['answer'], 'подробнее'), 'empty message does not ask for details');

$long = Chat::answer(str_repeat('а', 10000));
$check(is_array($long) && $long['rejected'] === true, 'long nonsense is not rejected');

$settingsModel = Settings::instance();
$servicesBackup = $settingsModel->getAttribute('chat_services');
$servicesUrlBackup = $settingsModel->getAttribute('chat_services_url');

$settingsModel->forceFill(['chat_services' => '', 'chat_services_url' => '']);

$neutral = Chat::answer('какие услуги вы оказываете');
$check(!str_contains($neutral['answer'], 'Elv Studio'), 'empty services still advertise the studio');
$check(($neutral['link'] ?? null) === null, 'empty services must not add a link');
$check(str_contains($neutral['answer'], 'обратной связи'), 'empty services do not point to feedback');
$check(!str_contains($neutral['answer'], 'В демо-записи'), 'services answer mixes booking services');

$neutralAudit = Chat::answer('аудит сайта');
$check(($neutralAudit['link'] ?? null) === null, 'audit without configured services must not link');

$settingsModel->forceFill(['chat_services' => "Стрижка\nМаникюр", 'chat_services_url' => '/uslugi/']);

$custom = Chat::answer('какие услуги вы оказываете');
$check(str_contains($custom['answer'], 'Стрижка') && str_contains($custom['answer'], 'Маникюр'), 'configured services are not listed');
$check(str_contains((string) ($custom['link']['url'] ?? ''), '/uslugi'), 'configured services link is not used');
$check(str_ends_with((string) ($custom['link']['url'] ?? ''), '/'), 'configured services link lost the trailing slash');

$settingsModel->forceFill(['chat_services' => "Стрижка\nМаникюр\nаудит"]);
$audit = Chat::answer('аудит сайта');
$check(str_contains((string) ($audit['link']['url'] ?? ''), '/uslugi'), 'audit link is missing when audit is listed');
$check(str_contains($audit['answer'], 'аудит'), 'audit answer is missing');

$settingsModel->forceFill(['chat_services' => $servicesBackup, 'chat_services_url' => $servicesUrlBackup]);

$booking = Chat::answer('записаться на услугу');
$check(str_contains($booking['answer'], 'Онлайн-запись'), 'booking answer is missing');

$contact = Chat::answer('связаться с вами');
$check($contact['rejected'] === false, 'contact request is rejected');
$check(!str_contains($contact['answer'], 'Не понял'), 'contact request falls into nonsense');

$setup = Chat::answer('как настроить сайт');
$check(str_contains($setup['answer'], 'Настройка сайта'), 'site setup answer is missing');

$pricing = Chat::answer('сколько стоит');
$check(str_contains($pricing['answer'], 'Тарифы Elv Kit'), 'pricing answer is missing');
$check(($pricing['link']['label'] ?? '') === 'Сравнить тарифы', 'pricing link is missing');
$check($pricing['sources'] === [], 'pricing answer must not add unrelated sources');
$check($pricing['rejected'] === false, 'valid pricing question must not be rejected');

$search = Chat::answer('найди статью про оплату');
$check(str_contains($search['answer'], 'Нашёл материалы'), 'explicit search answer is missing');
$check(count($search['sources']) >= 1, 'explicit search returns no sources');
$check(count($search['sources']) <= 3, 'explicit search returns more than three sources');
$check($search['rejected'] === false, 'valid search must not be rejected');

$keep = Chat::answer('как перестать получать письма');
$check(!str_contains($keep['answer'], 'Нашёл материалы'), 'word «перестать» is mistaken for a request about articles');
$check(!str_contains($keep['answer'], 'Блог входит'), 'word «перестать» is mistaken for a request about the blog');

$bundle = Chat::answer('покажи связку модулей');
$check(
    str_contains($bundle['answer'], 'Раздел') || str_contains($bundle['answer'], 'Модул'),
    'word «связка» is mistaken for a request to get in touch'
);

$urls = [];

foreach ($search['sources'] as $source) {
    $check(str_starts_with((string) ($source['url'] ?? ''), 'http'), 'source url is not absolute: ' . ($source['label'] ?? ''));
    $check(in_array($source['kind'] ?? '', ['Статья', 'Страница'], true), 'source kind is unknown: ' . ($source['kind'] ?? ''));
    $urls[] = $source['url'] ?? '';
}

$check(count($urls) === count(array_unique($urls)), 'search sources contain duplicates');

$fallback = Chat::answer('как настроить CRM');
$check(str_contains($fallback['answer'], 'Точного ответа нет'), 'fallback did not use site search');
$check(count($fallback['sources']) >= 1, 'fallback search returns no sources');
$check($fallback['rejected'] === false, 'fallback with sources must not be rejected');

$nothing = Chat::answer('найди абракадабру без совпадений');
$check(str_contains($nothing['answer'], 'ничего не нашёл'), 'empty search does not report the miss');
$check($nothing['open'] === 'feedback', 'empty search must offer feedback');
$check($nothing['sources'] === [], 'empty search must not expose sources');

if ($errors !== []) {
    foreach ($errors as $error) {
        fwrite(STDERR, 'FAIL: ' . $error . PHP_EOL);
    }

    exit(1);
}

echo 'OK: chat checks passed' . PHP_EOL;

===== FILE: tools/search-check.php =====

<?php

require __DIR__ . '/../bootstrap/autoload.php';

$app = require __DIR__ . '/../bootstrap/app.php';
$app->make(Illuminate\Contracts\Console\Kernel::class)->bootstrap();

use Elv\Kit\Classes\Search;

$errors = [];

$check = function (bool $condition, string $message) use (&$errors) {
    if (!$condition) {
        $errors[] = $message;
    }
};

$index = Search::index();
$check(count($index) >= 90, 'search index is smaller than expected');
$check(Search::index() === $index, 'search index cache returns a different array');

$longest = 0;
$hasLongText = false;

foreach ($index as $item) {
    $length = mb_strlen((string) ($item['text'] ?? ''));
    $longest = max($longest, $length);

    if ($length > 320) {
        $hasLongText = true;
    }

    $check($length <= 801, 'search text is longer than the index limit: ' . (string) ($item['url'] ?? ''));
}

$check($hasLongText, 'search index still truncates every article at 320 characters');
$check($longest <= 801, 'search index text limit is not 800 characters');

foreach (['оплата', 'оплатить', 'тарифами', 'цены', 'настройки', 'хостинг'] as $query) {
    $check(Search::query($query, Search::TYPE_ALL, 10) !== [], 'search returns nothing for: ' . $query);
}

$boundaryUrls = array_column(Search::query('став', Search::TYPE_ALL, 20), 'url');
$check(
    !in_array('/blog/sajt-dlya-lokalnoj-dostavki/', $boundaryUrls, true),
    'search matches «став» inside «доставка», not at a word start'
);
$check(
    array_intersect(['/blog/og-kartinki-na-php/', '/blog/kak-vybrat-hosting/'], $boundaryUrls) !== [],
    'search lost word-start matches for «став»'
);

$pricing = Search::query('тарифами', Search::TYPE_ALL, 10);
$pricingUrls = array_column($pricing, 'url');
$check(in_array('/pricing/', $pricingUrls, true), 'morphology does not find /pricing/ for «тарифами»');

$docs = Search::query('настройки', Search::TYPE_ALL, 10);
$docsUrls = array_column($docs, 'url');
$check(in_array('/docs/', $docsUrls, true), 'morphology does not find /docs/ for «настройки»');

Search::flush();
$check(Search::index() !== [], 'search index is empty after flush');

if ($errors !== []) {
    foreach ($errors as $error) {
        fwrite(STDERR, 'FAIL: ' . $error . PHP_EOL);
    }

    exit(1);
}

echo 'OK: search checks passed' . PHP_EOL;

===== FILE: tools/seo-check.php =====

<?php

require __DIR__ . '/../bootstrap/autoload.php';

$app = require __DIR__ . '/../bootstrap/app.php';
$app->make(Illuminate\Contracts\Console\Kernel::class)->bootstrap();

use Elv\Kit\Classes\Faq;
use Elv\Kit\Classes\Seo;
use Elv\Kit\Models\Post;
use Elv\Kit\Models\Settings;

$errors = [];

$check = function (bool $condition, string $message) use (&$errors) {
    if (!$condition) {
        $errors[] = $message;
    }
};

$method = new ReflectionMethod(Seo::class, 'faqNodeFromItems');
$method->setAccessible(true);

$empty = $method->invoke(null, null, []);
$check($empty === [], 'empty FAQ source still builds a FAQPage node');

$filled = $method->invoke(null, null, [
    ['q' => 'Вопрос', 'a' => 'Ответ'],
]);
$check(($filled['@type'] ?? '') === 'FAQPage', 'FAQPage node is missing for a filled source');
$check(count($filled['mainEntity'] ?? []) === 1, 'FAQPage mainEntity is empty for a filled source');

$robots = Seo::robots();
$check(str_contains($robots, 'Clean-param: q&category&tag'), 'robots.txt misses the global Clean-param');
$check(!str_contains($robots, 'Clean-param: q&category&tag /blog/'), 'robots.txt still scopes Clean-param to /blog/');

$defaultImage = Seo::base() . '/themes/elv-studio/assets/img/og-default.png';
Seo::setPost(null);
$check(Seo::image() === $defaultImage, 'og:image is not the default without an article');

$post = new Post();
$post->cover = 'graph';
Seo::setPost($post);
$check(Seo::image() === $defaultImage, 'icon cover is used as an og:image');

$check(Faq::items('calculator') === [], 'empty settings still return the calculator FAQ');
$check(Faq::post('kak-sdelat-sajt') === [], 'empty settings still return the article FAQ');

        fwrite(STDERR, 'FAIL: ' . $error . PHP_EOL);

<?php

$check = function (bool $condition, string $message) use (&$errors) {
    if (!$condition) {
        $errors[] = $message;
    }

DB::beginTransaction();

try {
    $date = date('Y-m-d', strtotime('+2 days'));
    $slot = Slot::create([
        'service_code' => 'zz-check',
        'date' => $date,
        'time' => '9:05',
        'capacity' => 2,
        'taken' => 0,
    ]);
    $check($slot->time === '09:05', 'slot time is not normalized to HH:MM');
    $check($slot->date === $date, 'slot date is not normalized to Y-m-d');

    try {
        Slot::create([
            'service_code' => 'zz-check',
            'date' => $date,
            'time' => '09:05',
            'capacity' => 2,
            'taken' => 0,
        ]);
    }
    catch (ValidationException $e) {
        $duplicate = true;

        Slot::create([
            'service_code' => 'zz-check',
            'date' => $date,
            'time' => '25:00',
            'capacity' => 1,
            'taken' => 0,
        ]);
    }
    catch (ValidationException $e) {
        $badTime = true;

            'service_code' => 'zz-check',
            'date' => $date,
            'time' => '10:00',
            'capacity' => 1,
            'taken' => 2,

        $overbooked = true;

    $slot->save();

    try {
        $slot->delete();
        $deleted = false;

        // Занятый слот удалять нельзя — это и проверяем.

    $slot->taken = 0;
    $slot->save();
    $slot->delete();
    $check(Slot::query()->find($id) === null, 'free slot is not deleted');

    $range = Slots::generateRange('ok', '2030-01-07', '2030-01-08', '10:00', '11:00', 60, 2, false);
    $check($range['created'] === 4, 'slot range generation did not create four slots');
    $check($range['total'] === 4, 'slot range generation reports the wrong total');

    $repeat = Slots::generateRange('ok', '2030-01-07', '2030-01-08', '10:00', '11:00', 60, 2, false);
    $check($repeat['created'] === 0, 'repeated slot generation creates duplicates');
    $check($repeat['skipped'] === 4, 'repeated slot generation does not skip existing slots');

    $generated = Slot::query()
        ->where('service_code', 'ok')
        ->whereBetween('date', ['2030-01-07', '2030-01-08'])
        ->get();
    $check($generated->count() === 4, 'generated slot count does not match');
    $check((int) ($generated->first()->capacity ?? 0) === 2, 'generated slot capacity is not applied');

    $reversed = false;

    try {
        Slots::generateRange('ok', '2030-01-08', '2030-01-07', '10:00', '11:00');
    }
    catch (\InvalidArgumentException $e) {
        $reversed = true;
    }

    $check($reversed, 'reversed slot range is not rejected');

    $overLimit = false;

    try {
        Slots::generateRange('ok', '2030-01-01', '2030-12-31', '10:00', '18:00', 5);
    }
    catch (\InvalidArgumentException $e) {
        $overLimit = true;
    }

    $check($overLimit, 'slot range over 500 is not rejected');

    $unknownService = false;

    try {
        Slots::generateRange('zz-no-such', '2030-01-07', '2030-01-08', '10:00', '11:00');
    }
    catch (\InvalidArgumentException $e) {
        $unknownService = true;
    }

    $check($unknownService, 'slot range accepts a service missing from settings');

    $check(BookingReminders::normalizeHours(0) === BookingReminders::DEFAULT_HOURS, 'zero reminder hours are not defaulted');
    $check(BookingReminders::normalizeHours(500) === 72, 'reminder hours are not capped at 72');
    $check(BookingReminders::normalizeHours(1) === 1, 'one hour reminder is not allowed');

    $settings->forceFill(['booking_reminder_enabled' => true, 'booking_reminder_hours' => 12]);
    $check(BookingReminders::enabled() === true, 'reminder switch is not read from settings');
    $check(BookingReminders::hours() === 12, 'reminder hours are not read from settings');

    $settings->forceFill(['booking_reminder_hours' => 500]);
    $check(BookingReminders::hours() === 72, 'reminder hours from settings are not capped');

    $now = \Carbon\Carbon::parse('2026-12-01 10:00:00');
    $makeBooking = function (array $attributes = []) {
        return Booking::create(array_merge([
            'service_code' => 'zz-check',
            'service' => 'Проверка',
            'price' => '0 BYN',
            'date' => '2026-12-01',
            'time' => '18:00',
            'person' => 'Тест',
            'email' => 'zz-check@example.com',
            'status' => Booking::STATUS_NEW,
        ], $attributes));
    };

    $due = $makeBooking();
    $makeBooking(['date' => '2026-12-03']);
    $makeBooking(['time' => '12:00', 'status' => Booking::STATUS_CANCELLED]);
    $makeBooking(['time' => '12:30', 'email' => '']);
    $alreadyReminded = $makeBooking(['time' => '13:00']);
    $alreadyReminded->reminded_at = $now->copy()->subHour();
    $alreadyReminded->save();

    $pending = BookingReminders::pending(24, $now);
    $check($pending->count() === 1, 'reminder selection does not return exactly the due booking');
    $check((int) ($pending->first()->id ?? 0) === (int) $due->id, 'reminder selection picked the wrong booking');
    $check(BookingReminders::moment($due) !== null, 'reminder moment is not parsed from date and time');
    $check(BookingReminders::moment($makeBooking(['time' => 'nonsense'])) === null, 'broken booking time still produces a moment');

    Mail::fake();
    $sent = BookingReminders::send(24, $now);
    $check($sent === 1, 'reminder send does not report one letter');
    $check($due->fresh()->reminded_at !== null, 'reminder mark is not saved');
    Mail::assertQueued(BookingReminder::class);
    $check(BookingReminders::pending(24, $now)->count() === 0, 'reminded booking is selected twice');

    $settings->forceFill(['booking_reminder_admin' => true]);
    $check(BookingReminders::pendingAdmin(24, $now)->count() === 3, 'admin reminder selection is wrong');
    $adminSent = BookingReminders::sendAdmin(24, $now);
    $check($adminSent === 3, 'admin reminder send does not report three letters');
    $check($due->fresh()->admin_reminded_at !== null, 'admin reminder mark is not saved');
    Mail::assertQueued(BookingReminderAdmin::class);
    $check(BookingReminders::pendingAdmin(24, $now)->count() === 0, 'admin reminder is sent twice');
}
finally {
    DB::rollBack();
}

$settings->forceFill(['booking_services' => $backup]);
$settings->forceFill([
    'booking_reminder_enabled' => $backupReminderEnabled,
    'booking_reminder_hours' => $backupReminderHours,
    'booking_reminder_admin' => $backupReminderAdmin,
]);

if ($errors !== []) {
    foreach ($errors as $error) {
        fwrite(STDERR, 'FAIL: ' . $error . PHP_EOL);
